pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    match crate::store_config::StoreConfig::resolve(options) {
        Ok(Some(config)) => return report("deploy", super::configured_release::deploy(options, config)),
        Err(error) => return report("deploy", Err(error)),
        Ok(None) => {},
    }
    let mut security_summary=None;
    let mut failure_inventory=None;
    let mut failure_problems=None;
    let result=(|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            crate::store_config::local_deployment(options, &dir)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let (_deployment, held) = if checking { (None, None) } else { let (owner, held) = activation_target(&dir)?; (owner, Some(held)) };
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema");
            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){
                failure_problems=Some(serde_json::json!({"problems":bound.diagnostics,"notes":bound.notes}));
                if !options.json{eprintln!("deploy verdict: REFUSED; compilation");super::report_diagnostics(&bound,false,false,true);}
                return Err("selected source does not compile".into())
            }
            let mut saved_version = super::saved_version::SavedVersion::prepare(&project,&sources,&bound,options,&dir)?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            let clock_notice=staging::clock_notice(&dir);
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {held.expect("live activation target status")};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            security_summary={
                let prior=previous.map(|n|bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))).transpose()?;
                super::security::release_loop(&project,&bound,&sources,prior.as_ref(),options)?
            };
            super::security::enforce_release(security_summary.as_ref(),options)?;
            let baseline=crate::client_compatibility::report(&project,&bound).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.1;
            let mut options=options.clone();
            options.flags.insert("client-baseline-advice".into(),serde_json::to_string(&baseline).unwrap());
            if let Some(summary)=&security_summary {options.flags.insert("security-summary".into(),summary.clone());}
            let options=&options;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            let staged_digest = crate::release_files::executable_digest(&stage)?;
            let mut matching = std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
                .filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e| e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok())
                .filter(|n| !super::rollback_views::is_view(&dir,*n) && selectable(&dir, *n).is_ok())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
            matching.sort_unstable_by(|a,b| b.cmp(a));
            let mut matched = None;
            for n in matching {
                if crate::release_files::executable_digest(&releases.join(n.to_string()))? == staged_digest { matched=Some(n); break; }
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound=bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            let parent=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
            let views=super::rollback_views::prepare(&dir,&stage,&staged_bound,parent)?;
            let register_views=super::rollback_views::needs_registration(&dir,matched,&views)?;
            if let Some(origin)=matched {check_change(&dir,previous,&bound,options,Some(origin))?;}
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if !register_views && matched==Some(previous) && held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version { saved.finish(previous,&source_hash)?; }
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"],"live":live_identity(&dir,previous)?}));}
                    else {println!("deploy verdict: UNCHANGED; {}",super::security::release_verdict(security_summary.as_ref()));print_live(&live_identity(&dir,previous)?);println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);if let Some(notice)=&clock_notice {eprintln!("{notice}");}}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            if let Some(n) = matched.filter(|_|!register_views) {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();
                if let Some(summary)=&security_summary {preview_options.flags.insert("security-summary".into(),summary.clone());}
                preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                preview_options.flags.insert("restore-dormant".into(),String::new());
                if !checking { preview_options.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new()); }
                let details=super::release_preview::plan(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),releases.join(n.to_string()),n,&preview_options,_deployment.as_ref())?;
                if checking { return Ok(()); }
                let mut with_details=options.clone();with_details.flags.insert("release-preview".into(),details.to_string());let options=&with_details;
                let selected = bound_at(releases.join(n.to_string()))?;
                activate(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),n,previous,revision,selected,false,true,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),options,None)?;
                if let Some(saved)=&mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash)?;}
                return Ok(());
            }
            let mut qualified_options=options.clone();
            if let Some(origin)=matched.filter(|_|register_views){qualified_options.flags.insert("source-release".into(),origin.to_string());qualified_options.flags.insert("restore-dormant".into(),String::new());}
            let options=&qualified_options;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();
                if let Some(summary)=&security_summary {preview_options.flags.insert("security-summary".into(),summary.clone());}preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options,None);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let mut validation=options.clone();
            validation.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
            validation.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new());
            let details=super::release_preview::plan(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&validation,_deployment.as_ref())?;
            let mut with_details=options.clone();with_details.flags.insert("release-preview".into(),details.to_string());let options=&with_details;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema { return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into()); }
            n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
            let final_dir=releases.join(n.to_string());
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let mut installed_views=super::rollback_views::install(&dir,&stage,n,&views)?;
            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut candidate=crate::release_files::Candidate::new(&dir,n);
            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let result=activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                n,
                previous,
                revision,
                staged_bound,
                false,
                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                Some(&mut candidate),
            );
            installed_views.pending=candidate.pending;
            match result {
                Ok(()) => {
                    candidate.committed=true;
                    if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");}
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash).map_err(|e|format!("release {n} committed, but saving its snapshot failed: {e}; retry unchanged deploy with --save-version"))?;}
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
                        loop {
                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
                        }
                        failure_inventory=Some(inventory);
                    }
                    return Err(error);
                }
            }
            if let Some(notice)=clock_notice {eprintln!("{notice}");}
            Ok(())
        })();
    if !options.json {
        if result.is_err() && failure_problems.is_none() {eprintln!("deploy verdict: REFUSED; {}",super::security::release_verdict(security_summary.as_ref()));}
        super::security::print_release(security_summary.as_ref());

    }
    if options.json {if let Err(error)=&result {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"message":error,"dormant":failure_inventory,"compilation":failure_problems,"security":security_summary.as_ref().and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok())}));}}
    report("deploy",result)
    if requested.exists() { crate::store_config::local_deployment(options, &requested)?; }
    if options.flags.contains_key("wait-stopped") {
        let dir = directory(options)?;
        crate::store_owner::wait_released(&dir.join("store.sqlite"), std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))?;
        println!("store released: {}", dir.display());
        return Ok(());
    }
    let requested_port = crate::port_flag::parse("serve", options.flags.get("port").map(String::as_str))?;
    let bind = release_bind(options)?;
    let capacity = crate::host_limits::Capacity::from_options(&options.flags, |name| {
        std::env::var(name).ok()
    })?;
    let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let owner = crate::store_owner::host(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))?;
    owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
    let clock_offset = options.flags.get("clock-offset").map(|text| crate::dev_clock::offset(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
    if options.flags.contains_key("clock-offset") {
        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if store.deployment_environment().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release().to_string()); }
    }
    let current = current(&dir, &owner)?.ok_or("no current release; deploy first")?;
    let release = Project {
        root: dir.join("releases").join(current.to_string())
            .canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    };
    crate::release_files::link_store(&release.root)?;
    let (port, listener) = crate::port_flag::listen("serve", requested_port, bind, None)?;
    let worker = options
        .flags
        .get("worker")
        .map(|path| {
            Path::new(path)
                .canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| format!("--worker {path}: {e}"))
        })
        .transpose()?;
    crate::serve::serve(
        release,
        port,
        crate::serve::Mode::Release {
            clock_offset,
            expected: current,
            worker,
            owner: std::sync::Arc::new(owner),
        },
        &bind.to_string(),
        listener,
        capacity,
    )
}

/// Ask the current retained owner to stop through its private same-owner
/// control socket, then wait until the store lock proves cleanup completed.
pub fn stop_release(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| {
        if crate::store_config::StoreConfig::resolve(options)?.is_some() {
            return Err("E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED: stop is outside the admitted store profile".into());
        }
        let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
        match crate::release_control::request(&dir, crate::release_control::Command::Stop) {
            Ok(_) => {
                crate::store_owner::wait_released(&dir.join("store.sqlite"), std::time::Duration::from_secs(10))?;
                println!("store stopped: {}", dir.display());
                Ok(())
            }
            Err(control) => match crate::store_owner::wait_released(&dir.join("store.sqlite"), std::time::Duration::ZERO) {
                Ok(()) => {
                    println!("store already stopped: {}", dir.display());
                    Ok(())
                }
                Err(_) => Err(control),
            },
        }
    })();
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("stop: {error}"); 1 }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn release_bind(options: &Options) -> Result<std::net::IpAddr, String> {
    let bind = options
        .flags
        .get("bind")
        .map(String::as_str)
//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#19-wave-28-output-and-witness-decisions-dx11 — gates lead; advisory questions are opt-in.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
mod record;
mod presence;
pub(super) mod witnesses;
#[cfg(test)] mod derived_tests;
pub(super) use presence::remember as remember_presence;
pub(super) use record::{remember,capture};
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(project,bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->Report{
    let augmented=if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){security::derived::augmented(sources,bound)}else{bound.clone()};
    let bound=&augmented;
    let mut selection=options.clone();
    selection.json=true; // Internal fixture loading must not print a second check transcript.
    let selected_fixture=record::selected_fixture(project,options);
    if selected_fixture.is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),selected_fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let inputs=match selection.flags.get("fixture"){
        Some(path)=>match super::test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{let mut report=security::analyze_rules(bound);security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);report.campaign_coverage.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));return report;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&inputs{selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let cached=record::matching(project,&selection,sources);
    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){let mut campaign=security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(10000);campaign.work_remaining=Some(8192);campaign}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)}};
    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    // An explicitly selected fixture remains the initial-state assumption.
    // Static generation runs only on the fixture-free path; both share this
    // one bounded campaign with all subsequent concrete exploration.
    let mut report=if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")||known_failure||options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){
        security::analyze_rules(bound)
    }else{security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign)};
    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{
                    // An empty starter directory selects generation, just as
                    // no fixture does. Give the direct contradiction its turn
                    // before empty-start mutation permutations spend the cap.
                    report=security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign);
                    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
                    security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)
                },
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,None,sources).unwrap_or_else(|why|vec![snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING",why,"repair the unreadable generated presence baseline").guide("security")]));
    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    let mut canonical=security::analyze_rules(bound);
    security::add_source_notes(&mut canonical,sources,bound);
    canonical.notes.extend(report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING").cloned());
    report.notes=canonical.notes;
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let sources=match project.sources(){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}};
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(snapback4_lang::compile(&sources),options.json);
    // Audit is a permission map plus bounded evidence; `test` owns full campaigns.
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&sources,options);
    let dispositions=match super::security_reviews::review(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None){Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{eprintln!("audit: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");return 2;}};
    if options.json {let mut value=serde_json::to_value(&report).unwrap();value["securityReviews"]=serde_json::to_value(&dispositions).unwrap();println!("{value}");}
    else {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);print!("{}",human.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));super::security_reviews::print(&dispositions,&report.notes);}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — report enforcement facts without claiming complete security.
pub(super) fn enforced(report:&Report,bound:&Bound){println!("{}",enforced_text(report,bound));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn enforced_text(report:&Report,bound:&Bound)->String {
    let roles=report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").count();
    let columns=bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(_,t)|t.column_reads.len()).sum::<usize>();
    let uploads=bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|t.columns.values()).filter(|t|t.contains_asset()).count();
    format!("check: enforced by the language — {uploads} typed upload columns with owner-bound placement; {columns} column audiences; {roles} possible self-assignable role paths detected (bounded analysis)")
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let failures=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted).count();
    let rule_enforced=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&v.direct_rule==security::Status::Proved).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted; {rule_enforced} enforced by rule (additional paths not proved); {} other unproved ({} failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted-rule_enforced,failures.len());
    for v in &failures {
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        eprintln!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap());
    }
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    if supplemental>0 {eprintln!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)");}
    if verbose {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);eprint!("{}",human.text_with_all(true));}
    else if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (check --verbose/JSON keeps every reason)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  review audiences and {} uncovered exposures: snapback4 audit",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options)->Result<Option<String>,String>{
    let changed=match prior {
        None=>true,
        Some(old)=>serde_json::to_vec(&old.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?,
    };
    if !changed{return Ok(None);}
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#153-rule-changing-deployment-review — review is prominent, strictness is explicit and failure-origin aware.
    let started=std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut preflight=options.clone();if !options.flags.contains_key("staging")||options.flags.contains_key("strict"){preflight.flags.insert("security-deploy".into(),String::new());}
    let mut report=report(project,bound,sources,&preflight);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,prior,sources)?);
    if super::proof_source::sources(project,options,"schema")?!=sources{return Err("selected Quarry sources changed during deployment security preflight".into());}
    let dispositions=super::security_reviews::review_snapshot(project,sources,&report.notes)?;
    let derived=security::derived::notes(sources,bound);
    let acknowledged=derived.iter().filter(|n|report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).any(|(r,d)|r.site==n.site&&r.code==n.code&&r.message==n.message&&d.acknowledged)).map(|n|n.site.to_string()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
    let pending=super::security_reviews::pending(&dispositions);
    let fixes=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())&&(!security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)||!acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()))).count();
    if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") { eprintln!("security deploy campaign: {} ms; bounded replay, remaining paths retain coverage gaps",started.elapsed().as_millis()); }
    let scope=release_scope(bound,prior,sources);
    let review=options.flags.contains_key("review")||options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
    let mut notice=String::new();
    for v in report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())){
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        if security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant)&&acknowledged.contains(&v.invariant.site.to_string()){
            if review {notice.push_str(&format!("  REVIEWED derived {}: individual specification decision retained\n",v.invariant.target_label()));} continue;
        }
        notice.push_str(&format!("  FIX {} {}: refuted via {} as {} ({:?}); repair enforcement to match intent, then run snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,c.reachability));
        if !security::derived::is_derived(&v.invariant) {
            if v.invariant.target=="write"&&v.invariant.column.is_some()&&c.effect=="insert" {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if broader creation is deliberate, replace this assertion with `invariant {}.{} update only {}`; then run snapback4 test. Authored contradictions cannot be cleared with --ack-security\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,v.invariant.column.as_ref().unwrap(),v.invariant.predicate));
            }else {
                notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if this observed audience is deliberate, revise the authored assertion here to match that specification, then run snapback4 test; --ack-security applies only to derived questions, never an authored contradiction\n",v.invariant.site));
            }
        }
        let aggregates=bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.source==v.invariant.table).map(|m|m.target.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if c.observations.is_some()&&!aggregates.is_empty()&&v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            notice.push_str(&format!("  DERIVED {}: a separately readable aggregate changes in the paired observation; tightening the source read rule alone cannot clear this disclosure\n",aggregates.join(", ")));
            let field=v.invariant.predicate.split('.').skip(1).filter_map(|part|part.split(|c:char|!c.is_alphanumeric()&&c!='_').next()).find(|field|bound.schema.table(&v.invariant.table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(*field)).is_some_and(|kind|matches!(kind.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)));
            if let Some(field)=field {notice.push_str(&format!("  INTENT {}: if aggregate counts are intentionally public, replace the whole-row assertion with `invariant {}.{} read only {}` for this protected field and independently declare any other private fields; otherwise restrict the aggregate audience; rerun snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.table,field,v.invariant.predicate));}
        }else if v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.invariant.column.is_none() {
            for (file,text) in sources {
                let doc=snapback4_lang::parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
                if let Some(table)=doc.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==v.invariant.table) {
                    if let Some(rule)=table.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==snapback4_lang::ast::Effect::Read) {
                        let expanded=bound.refusal_sites.get(&(v.invariant.table.clone(),"$read-rule".into()));
                        if expanded.is_some() {
                            notice.push_str(&format!("  SOURCE {}: preserve `{}` and its parent lifetime/authority; if this audience is unintended, repair the parent read rule before rerunning snapback4 test\n",v.invariant.table,snapback4_lang::bind::spell_internals(&snapback4_lang::print::expression(&rule.value))));
                        }else {

    notes.sort_by_key(|n|crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code));
    let count=notes.len();

    if review&&count>5 {notice.push_str(&format!("  {count} changed-scope notes in deploy --check --json\n"));}
    let selected=report.notes.iter().zip(&dispositions).filter(|(n,d)|d.gated || n.code!="N_SECURITY_INTENT" && scope.iter().any(|(file,start,end)| n.site.file==*file&&n.site.line>=*start&&n.site.line<*end)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let complete_family=dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.family.is_some()).count()==selected.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()).count();
    let scoped_notes=selected.iter().map(|(n,_)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let scoped_dispositions=selected.iter().map(|(_,d)| {let mut d=(*d).clone(); if !complete_family {d.family=None;} d}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // A scoped deploy cannot silently acknowledge unseen family members. It
    // prints their individual commands; ordinary check offers the whole family.
    notice.push_str(&super::security_reviews::render_mode(&scoped_dispositions,&scoped_notes,review));
    let test=release_notice(project,bound,sources,options);
    let advice=scoped_dispositions.iter().filter(|d|!d.gated).count();
    notice.push_str(&format!("{}; {advice} advisory decisions: deploy --review (or check --review)\n",enforced_text(&report,bound).replacen("check:","security:",1)));
    if review||test.contains("run `snapback4 test")||test.contains("FAILED") {notice.push_str(&format!("  {test}\n"));}
    let verdict=format!("security FIX {fixes}; {} pending review",dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged).count());
    Ok(Some(serde_json::json!({"notice":notice,"verdict":verdict,"fixes":fixes,"pending":pending,"changedNotes":count,"report":report,"securityReviews":dispositions}).to_string()))

fn age(timestamp:Option<i64>,now:i64)->String{
    timestamp.map(|t|format!("at {t} ms since Unix epoch, age {}s",now.saturating_sub(t).max(0)/1000)).unwrap_or_else(||"time/age unknown (legacy record)".into())
}
fn release_notice(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->String{
    let selection=options.flags.get("schema").map(|s|format!(" --source {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let fixture=options.flags.get("fixture").map(|s|format!(" --fixture {s:?}")).unwrap_or_default();
    let now=snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let matching=record::matching(project,options,sources);
    let matches=matching.is_some();
    match matching.or_else(||record::latest(project)){
        Some(record)=>{
            let result=if !record.complete{"incomplete"}else if record.run_violations.unwrap_or(record.violations.len())>0{"FAILED"}else{"completed, no observed violation"};
            let current=if matches{"matches selected source/fixtures/CLI"}else{"STALE: source, fixtures or CLI differ; does not qualify selected release"};
            let next=if matches&&record.complete&&record.run_violations.unwrap_or(record.violations.len())==0{"matching test campaign recorded (finite, not proof)".into()}else{format!("run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment")};
            format!("last test: {result}, {}; {current}; {} retained reachable refutations, {} coverage gaps; last completed test {}; {next}",age(record.recorded_at,now),record.violations.len(),record.coverage_gaps,age(record.last_completed_at,now))
        },
        None=>format!("last test: NEVER RECORDED; run `snapback4 test{selection}{fixture}` before deployment; no matching completed campaign for this source, fixtures and CLI; {} independent invariants",bound.invariants.len()),
    }
}

function validatePlan(options: SeedAccountsOptions) {

  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeout) || timeout < 1 || !roles.length || roles.some(([role, n]) => !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(role) || !Number.isSafeInteger(n) || n < 1)

  }

}

async function accounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions, environment: "staging" | "production" | "development", roster?: Roster, save: () => void = () => {}): Promise<StagingSessions> {
  const {service, roles, timeout} = validatePlan(options);

  const remaining = () => {

    return ms;
  };
  const request = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => fetch(host.url + path, { ...init, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(remaining(), 30_000)) });
  const config = await request("/auth/config");
  const identity = await config.json();
  if (!config.ok || identity.environment !== environment || identity.development !== (environment === "development") || !identity.password) {
    throw Error(environment === "development" ? "E_DEV_ACCOUNTS: devAccounts requires startDev with password identity; serve hosts refuse before creating users" : environment === "staging"
      ? "E_STAGING: account seeding requires serve on a fresh deploy --staging with password identity; production and dev refuse before creating users"
      : "E_PRODUCTION_ACCOUNTS: account provisioning requires serve on a production deploy with password identity; staging and dev refuse before creating users");
  }

  if (environment === "production") {

    const named = response.ok && Array.isArray(schema.service_identities) && schema.service_identities.includes(`service:${service}`);
    if (!named) process.stderr.write(`snapback4: WARNING minted service:${service}, but the served schema reports no explicit positive authorization mention for that service identity; rules still decide every operation and provisionAccounts(${options.provision.name}) will likely refuse.\n`);
  }

  for (const [role, count] of roles) {
    sessions[role] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let fresh = false;
      let entry = roster?.entries.find(entry => entry.role === role && entry.index === i);
      if (!entry) {
        fresh = true;
        entry = { role, index: i, email: `${environment}-${role}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`, password: randomBytes(24).toString("base64url"), id: randomUUID(), done: false };
        roster?.entries.push(entry); save(); // Persist identity before the first possibly ambiguous write.
      }
      const { email, password } = entry;
      let reply: Record<string, unknown>;
      if (roster && !fresh) {
        const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/login", {
          method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
        }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
          if ((error as {code?:string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: login deadline expired; ${partial}`);
          remaining(); throw error;
        });

      } else reply = {};
      if (!reply.session) {
      const result = await identityRequest(host.url + "/auth/signup", {
        method:"POST", headers:{"content-type":"application/json"}, body:JSON.stringify({email,password}),
      }, fetch, {timeoutMs:remaining()}).catch(error => {
        if ((error as {code?: string})?.code === "E_IDENTITY_TIMEOUT") throw Error(`E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: signup deadline expired; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
        remaining(); throw error;
      });
      reply = result.json;
      }
      if ((reply.denied as {code?:string} | undefined)?.code === "E_RATE_LIMIT") {
        throw Error("E_STAGING_TIMEOUT: identity retry budget exhausted; increase timeoutMs or inspect partial accounts/grants before retrying");
      }
      const session = reply.session as {principal?:string;kind?:string;token?:string;expiresAt?:number} | undefined;
      if (typeof session?.principal !== "string" || typeof session.token !== "string" || typeof session.expiresAt !== "number" || session.kind !== "user") {

      }
      const account: StagingAccount = { kind: session.kind, role, email, password, principal: session.principal, token: session.token, expiresAt: session.expiresAt };
      if (entry.principal && entry.principal !== account.principal) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: principal differs from saved roster; inspect deployment");
      entry.principal = account.principal; save();
      const args = { principal: account.principal, role, ...options.args?.(account) };
      const encodedArgs = canonical(args);
      if (entry.args && entry.args !== encodedArgs) throw Error("E_PRODUCTION_ROSTER: provisioning arguments changed; inspect roster instead of silently reassigning roles");
      entry.args = encodedArgs; save();
      if (!entry.done) {
      const response = await request(`/m/${options.provision.name}`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${operator}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ id: entry.id, args }) });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok || result.state !== "sent") throw Error(`E_STAGING_PROVISION: ${options.provision.name} refused (${result.why?.code ?? result.denied?.code ?? "invalid reply"}); supplied arguments: ${Object.keys(args).sort().join(", ")}; default provision arguments are principal and role; use args(account) for custom names; partial accounts/grants may exist; ${partial}`);
      entry.done = true; save();
      }
      sessions[role]!.push(account);
    }
  }
  return sessions;
}

/** @throws E_STAGING_INPUT for invalid plans; E_STAGING for production/dev hosts; E_STAGING_TIMEOUT, E_STAGING_SIGNUP or E_STAGING_PROVISION for incomplete setup. */
export function seedAccounts(host: Pick<ServeHost, "url" | "operatorToken">, options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions> {

fn completed_verdict(options:&Options) {
    println!("{} verdict: READY / completed; {}",options.flags.get("output-verb").map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("deploy"),super::security::release_verdict(options.flags.get("security-summary")));
}
fn receipt(release:u64,generation:u64,report:&snapback4_core::store_port::report::AdoptionReport,options:&Options,live:&serde_json::Value){
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"live":live,"sourceRelease":options.flags.get("source-release").and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()),"release":release,"generation":generation,"adoption":report.compact(),"details":report.summary(),"backfills":report.backfills,"security":options.flags.get("security-summary").and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()),"rollbackView":options.flags.get("rollback-view").and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()),"preview":options.flags.get("release-preview").and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok())}));}
    else {completed_verdict(options);print_live(live);println!("release {release} is current as generation {generation}; {}",report.compact());if let Some(origin)=options.flags.get("source-release"){println!("  retained source {origin} requalified as release {release} to register its current rollback views");}for fill in &report.backfills {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}if options.flags.contains_key("verbose"){println!("{}",report.summary());}}
}

        .filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|!super::rollback_views::is_view(dir,*n)&&selectable(dir,*n).is_ok()))

pub(super) fn print_release(summary:Option<&String>){if let Some(s)=summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()){eprintln!("{}",s["notice"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}}
pub(super) fn release_verdict(summary:Option<&String>)->String {summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok()).and_then(|s|s["verdict"].as_str().map(str::to_owned)).unwrap_or_else(||"security unchanged".into())}

pub(super) fn enforce_release(summary:Option<&String>,options:&Options)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(value)=summary.and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(s).ok())else{return Ok(())};
    if !options.flags.contains_key("staging")||options.flags.contains_key("strict"){
        if value["fixes"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0)>0{return Err("E_SECURITY_REVIEW: production deploy refuses reachable FIX; repair the named rule and rerun snapback4 test; for a derived candidate only, run check --ack-security ID --reason '<specification rationale>' using the reported rule ID".into())}
        if value["pending"].as_bool()==Some(true){return Err("E_SECURITY_REVIEW: production deploy requires individual security review; run each reported check --ack-security ID --reason '<specification decision>', or enforce the named intent in source".into())}
    }Ok(())
}

const substringLocators = new WeakMap<Locator, { locator: Locator; scoped: boolean }>();
/** Match the smallest reachable element whose composed descendant text matches; scroll a unique candidate into view when observed.
 * Strings normalize whitespace and match substrings by default; regexes keep their
 * own semantics. Editable values never count. Independent matches remain
 * ambiguous, including repeated rows. */
/** @throws None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. */
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#15-wave-26-proof-ergonomics-dx9 — substrings retain strict ambiguity and explicit exactness.
export function renderedText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp, options: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean } | null = {}): Locator {
  const normalized = options ?? {};
  const root = normalized.within ?? page;
  const match = (exact: boolean) => registeredLocator(root.locator(`${renderedSelector}=${JSON.stringify({
    source: typeof text === "string" ? text : text.source,
    flags: typeof text === "string" ? "" : text.flags,
    regex: typeof text !== "string", exact,
  })}`));
  const locator = match(normalized.exact ?? false);
  if (normalized.within) scopedLocators.add(locator);
  if (typeof text === "string" && normalized.exact === true) substringLocators.set(locator, { locator: match(false), scoped: normalized.within !== undefined });
  return locator;
}

export interface ExhaustPagesOptions {
  /** Refuse rather than loop forever when a backend returns bad continuations. */
  maxPages?: number;
  /** Names the read in a refusal without exposing its rows or cursor. */
  label?: string;
}
type ProofPage<Row> =
  | { readonly data: readonly Row[]; readonly next?: Cursor | null; readonly complete: boolean; readonly cut: ReadCut; readonly existence?: "present" | "absent" | "partial" }
  | { readonly denied: Refusal }
  | { readonly loading: true };
/** Read every page by passing each opaque `next` back unchanged. The callback
 * receives null first. Refusals, loading, malformed/repeated cursors and a
 * capped or incomplete page fail instead of returning a partial snapshot. */
/** @throws E_PROOF_PAGE for a refused, loading, malformed, repeated, incomplete or over-limit traversal; callback errors propagate. */
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#10-wave-25-day-two-friction-dx8 — proof traversal must never promote capped or partial reads to complete evidence.
export async function exhaustPages<Row>(readPage: (cursor: Cursor | null) => Promise<ProofPage<Row>>, options: ExhaustPagesOptions = {}): Promise<Row[]> {
  const maxPages = options.maxPages ?? 1_000, label = options.label ?? "paged read";
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxPages) || maxPages < 1) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: maxPages must be a positive safe integer`);
  const rows: Row[] = [], seen = new Set<string>();
  let cursor: Cursor | null = null;
  for (let pageNumber = 1; pageNumber <= maxPages; pageNumber++) {
    const page = await readPage(cursor);
    if (!page || typeof page !== "object") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned no read envelope`);
    if ("denied" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} refused ${page.denied.code}: ${page.denied.message}`);
    if ("loading" in page) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is still loading`);
    if (!Array.isArray(page.data)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} data is not an array`);
    if (page.cut !== null) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (${page.cut === undefined ? "missing" : page.cut} cut)`);
    if (page.complete !== true) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} is incomplete (complete must be true)`);
    if (page.existence === "partial") throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} has partial existence`);
    rows.push(...page.data);
    const next = page.next ?? null;
    if (next === null) return rows;
    if (typeof next !== "string" || next.length === 0) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a malformed cursor`);
    if (seen.has(next)) throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: page ${pageNumber} returned a repeated cursor`);
    seen.add(next);
    cursor = next as Cursor;
  }
  throw new Error(`E_PROOF_PAGE: ${label}: exceeded maxPages ${maxPages}`);
}
/** Activate before taking a screenshot, particularly on loaded shared hosts. */
/** @throws page closed or browser activation failed. */
export async function frontPage(page: Page): Promise<Page> { await page.bringToFront(); return page; }

/** Returns normalized visible nonempty text after it differs from previous. Hidden and editable descendants do not confer freshness. */
/** @throws observation deadline with scoped locator, last text and visible headings; selector ambiguity is propagated. */
export async function waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string | null, options: number | { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number } = 30_000): Promise<string> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(typeof options === "number" ? options : options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  let substring = substringLocators.get(locator);
  locator = registeredLocator(locator);
  if (typeof options !== "number" && options.within) {
    if (scopedLocators.has(locator)) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: within was supplied twice; scope renderedText or waitForFresh once");
    locator = registeredLocator(options.within.locator(locator));
    if (substring) substring = { locator: options.within.locator(substring.locator), scoped: true };
  }
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let count = 0, text: string | null = null, detail = "";
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    try {
      count = await bounded(locator.count(), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator count");
      if (count > 1) throw Error(`E_PROOF_AMBIGUOUS: strict mode violation: ${count} rendered matches; use a narrower within or exact: true: ${String(locator)}`);
      text = count ? await bounded(visibleText(locator, Math.max(1, Math.min(1000, end - performance.now()))), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()), "locator text") : null;
      if (text && text !== previous) return text;
    } catch (error) {
      detail = error instanceof Error && (error.name === "TimeoutError" || /timed out after/.test(error.message))
        ? "locator observation exceeded the remaining proof deadline" : String(error);
      // A strictness/selector error needs its original repair, not a timeout.
      if (error instanceof Error && error.name !== "TimeoutError" && !/timed out after/.test(error.message)) throw error;
    }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  const hint = count === 0 && substring?.scoped && await bounded(substring.locator.count(), 1000, "substring count").catch(() => 0)
    ? "; a substring match exists; pass a RegExp or `exact: false`" : "";
  const headings = await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => locator.page().locator('h1:visible, h2:visible, [role="heading"]:visible').allTextContents()), 1000, "visible headings").catch(() => []);
  const heading = headings.map(text => text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3).join(" | ").slice(0, 300);
  throw Error(`no fresh signal after ${timeoutMs} ms: ${String(locator)}; last count=${count}, text=${JSON.stringify(text)}; ${count ? "present but unchanged" : "absent"}; previous=${JSON.stringify(previous)}; visible heading=${JSON.stringify(heading || "(none)")}${hint}${detail ? `; ${detail}` : ""}`);
}
/** Poll authoritative server truth after a receipt; a pending prediction is not a commit. */
/** @throws deadline naming label and last value; read or predicate errors propagate. */
export async function waitForServer<T>(read: () => Promise<T>, accepts: (value: T) => boolean, options: { timeoutMs?: number; label?: string } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000), end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let last: T | undefined;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    try {
      const value = await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(read), new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
        timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`)), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()));
      })]);
      last = value;
      if (accepts(value)) return value;
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  throw Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`);
}
/** Wait until the page's own client has sampled an advanced server clock. Does not advance or replace that clock. */
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for invalid advanced time; missing/disabled witness, signed-out client, page error or page-clock deadline. */
export async function waitForClock(page: Page, advancedNow: number, options: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<number> {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(advancedNow) || advancedNow < 0) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: waitForClock requires the clock advance reply's now");
  return waitForServer(() => page.evaluate(() => {
    const witness = (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: { clock(): number } }).__snapback4Witness;
    if (!witness?.clock) throw Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: registerWebWitness and use witness=web before waitForClock");
    return witness.clock();
  }), now => now >= advancedNow, { ...options, label: "page client clock" });
}
/** @throws locator ambiguity, disabled/closed control, or Playwright deadline. */
export async function fillRN(locator: Locator, value: string | Parameters<Locator["setInputFiles"]>[0]): Promise<void> {
  const type = await locator.evaluate(element => element instanceof HTMLInputElement ? element.type : "text");
  if (type === "file") { await locator.setInputFiles(value); return; }
  if (typeof value !== "string") throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: file paths/payloads require a file input");
  if (["date", "datetime-local", "time", "month", "week"].includes(type)) { await locator.fill(value); return; }
  await locator.focus(); await locator.press("ControlOrMeta+A"); await locator.press("Backspace");
  await locator.pressSequentially(value);
}

export function statusText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp = /^(?:Ready|pending|sent|failed)$/): Locator { return renderedText(page, text); }

import { after as teardownAfter } from "./helpers/teardown.mjs";
// H9 controls can load the unchanged 0.4.29 proof/witness modules via KIT2_BASELINE_DIST.
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { chromiumExecutable } from '../../../witness/lib.mjs';
const dist = process.env.KIT2_BASELINE_DIST ?? resolve(import.meta.dirname, '../dist');
const kit = await import(pathToFileURL(join(dist, 'proof.js')));
const witnessSource = readFileSync(join(dist, 'witness-web.js'), 'utf8');
async function browser(t, run) {
  const cwd = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'kit2-contract-'));
  teardownAfter(t, () => rmSync(cwd, {recursive:true,force:true}));
  await kit.withBrowser({cwd, executablePath:process.env.CHROME ?? chromiumExecutable()}, async context => run(await context.newPage(), cwd));
}
test('DX9 renderedText defaults to substring matching for a row with more than the needle', {timeout:30000}, async t => browser(t, async page => {
  await page.setContent('<section aria-label="Rows"><p>Proof needle — more row text</p><input value="Proof needle"></section>');
  const within = page.getByRole('region', {name:'Rows'});
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'Proof needle — more row text', null).count(), 1, 'null options mean the defaults');
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'Proof needle', {within, exact:true}).count(), 0, 'exact remains opt-in');
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(kit.renderedText(page, 'Proof needle', {within, exact:true}), null, 50), /a substring match exists; pass a RegExp or `exact: false`/);
  const row = kit.renderedText(page, 'Proof needle', {within, exact:false});
  assert.equal(await row.count(), 1, 'explicit substring matches the whole rendered row');
  assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(row, null, {timeoutMs:500}), 'Proof needle — more row text');
  assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(kit.renderedText(page, /Proof needle/, {within}), null, 500), 'Proof needle — more row text');
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(row, 'Proof needle — more row text', {timeoutMs:1000}), /locator.*last count=1.*Proof needle.*present but unchanged/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(kit.renderedText(page,'missing'), null, {timeoutMs:1000}), /last count=0.*text=null.*absent/);
}));
test('DX8 renderedText finds a full status line anywhere in a scrollable RN-style root', {timeout:30000}, async t => browser(t, async page => {

  await page.setContent(`<main style="height:90px;overflow-y:auto"><div style="height:600px"></div><div><span>Pump </span><span>logged.</span></div></main>`);
  const root=page.locator('main');
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,'Pump logged.',{within:root,exact:false}).count(),1);
  assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(kit.renderedText(page,'Pump logged.',{within:root,exact:false}),null,500),'Pump logged.');
  assert.ok(await root.evaluate(element=>element.scrollTop)>0,'proof observation scrolls the match into view');
}));

test('DX8 exhaustPages follows opaque cursors and refuses incomplete traversal', async () => {
  const cursors=[];
  const rows=await kit.exhaustPages(async cursor=>{
    cursors.push(cursor);
    if(cursor===null)return {data:[{id:'newest'}],next:'opaque:page-2',complete:true,cut:null};
    return {data:[{id:'older'}],next:null,complete:true,cut:null};
  });
  assert.deepEqual(cursors,[null,'opaque:page-2']);
  assert.deepEqual(rows,[{id:'newest'},{id:'older'}]);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({denied:{code:'PRIVATE',message:'no'}})),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*PRIVATE/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({loading:true})),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*loading/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({data:[],next:null,cut:null})),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*complete must be true/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({data:[],next:'would-continue',complete:false,cut:'meter'})),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*incomplete.*meter/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({data:[],next:null,complete:true,cut:null,existence:'partial'})),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*partial existence/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.exhaustPages(async()=>({data:[],next:'same',complete:true,cut:null}),{maxPages:2,label:'board'}),/E_PROOF_PAGE.*board.*repeated cursor/);
});
test('DX6 composed RN text and wrapping labels preserve scope, exactness and ambiguity', {timeout:30000}, async t => browser(t, async page => {
  await page.setContent(`<section aria-label="Rows"><div><span>Order </span><span>42</span><span> saved</span></div>
    <label><span>Due </span><strong>tomorrow</strong><input value="secret value"></label>
    <p hidden>Order 42 saved</p><p>Order 42 saved later</p></section>`);
  const within = page.getByRole('region', {name:'Rows'});
  for (const text of ['Order 42 saved', /^Order 42 saved$/]) {
    const found = kit.renderedText(page, text, {within,exact:true});
    assert.equal(await found.count(), 1, 'composed text selects the smallest rendered container');
    assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(found, null, 500), 'Order 42 saved');
  }
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'Due tomorrow', {within}).count(), 1, 'wrapping input label composes descendants without its value');
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,'Due tomorrow',{within:page.locator('label')}).count(),1,'a wrapping label can itself be the scope, as with Playwright getByText');
  assert.equal(await kit.statusText(page, 'Due tomorrow').count(), 1);
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'secret value', {within}).count(), 0);
  await page.getByRole('region',{name:'Rows'}).evaluate(el=>el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<style>.invisible{display:none}</style><div><span hidden>Approved</span><button>Save</button></div><div><span class="invisible">Accepted</span><button>Submit</button></div><div><span style="visibility:hidden">Confirmed</span><button>Go</button></div><div><span style="opacity:0">Done</span><button>Finish</button></div><div contenteditable="true">Editable</div><div><span>Visible </span><span hidden>secret </span><strong>composed</strong></div>`));
  for(const text of ['Approved','Accepted','Confirmed','Done','Editable']) for(const needle of [text,new RegExp(text)]) assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,needle,{within}).count(),0,`${text}: hidden or editable text is not evidence`);
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,'Visible composed',{within}).count(),1,'hidden adornment does not prevent composed visible evidence');
  await page.getByRole('region',{name:'Rows'}).evaluate(el=>el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', `<div><span style="display:block;width:0;height:0;overflow:hidden">Submitted</span><button>Continue</button></div><div><span style="display:block;width:0;height:0;overflow:scroll">Finished</span></div><div><span style="display:block;width:0;height:0;border:100px solid transparent;overflow:hidden">Authorized</span></div><div style="width:10px;overflow:hidden;white-space:nowrap;font:20px monospace">Completed</div><p><span style="display:contents;overflow:hidden">Layoutless </span><span style="display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;overflow:visible">wrapper</span></p>`));
  for(const text of ['Submitted','Finished','Completed','Authorized']) assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,text,{within}).count(),0,`${text}: clipped text cannot manufacture full rendered evidence`);
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page,'Layoutless wrapper',{within}).count(),1,'layoutless and zero-size visible-overflow wrappers retain rendered text');
  const clipped=within.locator('div').filter({has:page.locator('span').filter({hasText:'Submitted'})}).first();
  assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(clipped,null,500),'Continue','arbitrary locator freshness uses the same clipping rules');
  const visible=kit.renderedText(page,'Visible composed',{within});
  assert.equal(await kit.waitForFresh(visible,null,500),'Visible composed');
  await visible.evaluate(el=>el.querySelector('[hidden]').textContent='changed invisible text');
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(visible,'Visible composed',50),/present but unchanged/);
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(visible.filter({visible:true}),'Visible composed',50),/present but unchanged/);


  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'order 42 saved', {within,exact:true}).count(), 0, 'exact remains case sensitive');
  assert.equal(await kit.renderedText(page, 'Order 42 saved', {within, exact:false}).count(), 2);
  await page.getByRole('region', {name:'Rows'}).evaluate(el => el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<p>Order 42 saved</p>'));
  await assert.rejects(kit.waitForFresh(kit.renderedText(page, 'Order 42 saved', {within}), null, 500), /strict mode violation/);
//! Minimal public release lifecycle over the resolved store configuration.
// @ref LLP 3000.009.001.000#initial-http-profile-and-public-release-bootstrap — immutable local source, persisted selection, acquired ownership.
use super::*;
use crate::store_config::StoreConfig;
use std::{path::Path, sync::Arc};

pub(super) fn deploy(options: &Options, config: StoreConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
    for flag in [
        "check",
        "staging",
        "save-version",
        "allow-destructive",
        "allow-feed-adoption",
    ] {
        if options.flags.contains_key(flag) {
            return Err(format!(
                "E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED: release-{flag} is outside the admitted store profile"
            ));
        }
    }
    let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
    let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(
        super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"),
        options.json,
    );
    if crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some()
        || !crate::dev_worker::declared(&project)?.is_empty()
    {
        return Err("E_STORE_UNSUPPORTED: workers are outside the admitted store profile".into());
    }
    // Validate profile before creating a schema, release tree or durable activation.
    let dir = super::releases::canonical_directory(options)?;
    if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {
        return Err("E_STORE_CONFIG: deployment already selects a local store".into());
    }
    let _command = super::releases::command_lock(&dir)?;
    let mut state = config.probe_for_deploy(&dir)?;
    if let Some(state) = &state {
        if dir.join("store-binding.json").is_file() {
            config.validate_binding(&dir, state)?;
        } else {
            config.recover(&dir, state)?;
        }
    }
    let prior = state
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|s| s.release)
        .map(|n| super::releases::bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string())))
        .transpose()?;
    super::security::release_loop(&project, &bound, &sources, prior.as_ref(), options)?;
    let programs = bound
        .programs
        .iter()
        .map(|p| (p.program.name.clone(), p.program.clone()))
        .collect();
    if state.is_none() {
        // The intent is durable before the bootstrap transaction. Creating its
        // handle makes no database writes; Engine admission precedes bootstrap.
        let id = config.pending(&dir, true)?;
        let store = config.create(&id)?;
        store
            .admit(&bound.schema, &programs)
            .map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?;
        // Bootstrap establishes the logical identity and initial generation only.
        // Selection/history below always run under a positive acquired token.
        let bootstrap = snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(
            store,
            bound.schema.clone(),
            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
            snapback4_core::now_ms(),
        )
        .map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?;
        let observed = bootstrap
            .store()
            .store_identity()
            .map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?
            .ok_or("store identity absent")?;
        if observed != id {
            return Err("E_STORE_CONFIG: bootstrap identity differs from pending intent; restore the original store and retry deploy".into());
        }
        drop(bootstrap);
        state = config.probe()?;
        config.recover(&dir, state.as_ref().ok_or("bootstrap did not establish store identity")?)?;
    }
    let state = state.ok_or("bootstrap did not establish store identity")?;
    let owner = config.acquire(&state.store_id)?;
    let store = owner.open().map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?;
    store
        .admit(&bound.schema, &programs)
        .map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?;
    let previous = store
        .release_value()
        .map_err(crate::store_config::redact)?
        .map(|r| r.parse::<u64>().map_err(|_| "invalid persisted release"))
        .transpose()?;
    if let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = super::releases::bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        if old.schema == bound.schema
            && old
                .programs
                .iter()
                .map(|p| &p.program)
                .eq(bound.programs.iter().map(|p| &p.program))
        {
            if options.json {
                println!(
                    "{}",
                    serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":n,"generation":state.generation,"store_id":state.store_id})
                );
            } else {
                println!(
                    "unchanged; release {n} stays current as generation {}",
                    state.generation
                );
//! A family command expands to current individual, source-bound records.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — review questions lead, and gates never enter the audience family.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::Diagnostic;

pub(super) fn audience(code: &str) -> bool {
    !gated(code) && matches!(code, "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_BROAD_READ")
}
pub(super) fn family(notes: &[Diagnostic], identities: &[(String,String,String)]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut members = notes.iter().zip(identities).filter(|(note,_)| audience(&note.code))
        .map(|(_, (id,source,_))| (id,source)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if members.is_empty() { return None; }
    members.sort();
    Some(format!("audience:{}", hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(ANALYZER,members)).unwrap())))
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn count(dispositions: &[Disposition]) -> usize {
    let audience = dispositions.iter().any(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_some());
    usize::from(audience) + dispositions.iter().filter(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_none()
        && crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count()
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn print(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) {print!("{}",render(dispositions,notes));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) -> String {
    use std::fmt::Write;
    let mut output=String::new();
    let mut ordered=notes.iter().zip(dispositions).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#165-review-clarification-exact-detector-and-command-boundaries — grouping must preserve gate/authority priority.
    ordered.sort_by_key(|(n,_)|(crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code),n.site.file.clone(),n.site.line,n.site.col));
    let members=ordered.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()&&!d.acknowledged).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut family_output=String::new();
    if let Some((_,first))=members.first().copied() {
        let codes=members.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.code.as_str()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"decision audience ({codes}): Are these {} row/column audiences, roles and lifecycle states intended?",members.len()).unwrap();
        for (n,_) in &members {
            // The expanded branch cross-product stays in JSON. One question
            // names the source subject without repeating every equivalent path.
            let subject = n.message.split(" matches or weakens").next().unwrap_or(&n.message)
                .split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
            writeln!(&mut family_output,"  ? {}: Is {} intended?",n.site,subject.trim_end_matches('.')).unwrap();
        }
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",first.family.as_deref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Narrower: restrict the listed rules; declare independent intent; run snapback4 test. Full audiences: check --json.").unwrap();
    }
    let mut family_printed=false;
    for (n,d) in ordered {
        if d.acknowledged {if let Some(w)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness recorded: {}",w["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("see check --json")).unwrap();} writeln!(&mut output,"security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("")).unwrap(); continue; }
        if d.family.is_some() {
            if !family_printed {output.push_str(&family_output);family_printed=true;}
            continue;
        }
        if !d.gated && crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review { continue; }
        let subject=n.message.split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
        let concise=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>format!("{subject}: should operation results/refusals expose protected or projected-out columns to every admitted caller"),
            _=>n.message.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']).to_owned(),
        };
        let concise=concise.replace("insert/upsert", "insert, or edit an existing row via upsert");
        let question=concise.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']);
        writeln!(&mut output,"decision {} at {}: {question}; is this intended?",d.code,d.site).unwrap();
        if let Some(witness)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: {}",witness["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("concrete campaign behavior; check --json for replay")).unwrap();}
        else if matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED"|"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"|"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"|"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY") {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: none obtained in this bounded campaign; `check --review` searches for concrete behavior; this remains a specification question").unwrap();}
        let repair=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>"restrict result/refusal audiences with column rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test",
            "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"=>"validate the recipient's current role and relationship; caller authority alone is insufficient",
            _=>n.rewrite.as_str(),
        };
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Repair: {repair}").unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'{}",d.id,if d.gated {" (required before check can pass)"}else{""}).unwrap();
    }
    output
}

/// One mode selector for the CLI and captured-analyzer format replays.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render_mode(dispositions:&[Disposition],notes:&[Diagnostic],review:bool)->String {
    let selected=dispositions.iter().zip(notes).filter(|(d,_)|review || d.recorded || d.gated&&!d.acknowledged).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    render(&selected.iter().map(|(d,_)|(*d).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),&selected.iter().map(|(_,n)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())
}
#[cfg(test)]mod tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test]fn authority_and_gates_precede_one_complete_audience_family(){
  let codes=["N_BROAD_READ","N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION","N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"];
  let notes=codes.iter().map(|code|Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"subject: review authority","repair enforcement")).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let dispositions=codes.iter().map(|code|Disposition{family:audience(code).then(||"audience:family".into()),id:(*code).into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:gated(code),acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let text=render(&dispositions,&notes);
  assert!(text.find("decision N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE").unwrap()<text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert!(text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap()<text.find("decision audience").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert_eq!(text.matches("decision audience").count(),1,"{text}");
  assert!(text.contains("Are these 2 row/column audiences")&&text.contains("audience:family"),"{text}");
 }
 #[test]fn every_gate_is_one_decision_and_never_a_family_member(){
  for code in snapback4_lang::security::GATED_NOTE_CODES {
   assert!(!audience(code));
   let n=Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"explicit review required","repair enforcement");
   let d=Disposition{family:None,id:"id".into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:true,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()};
   let text=render(&[d],&[n]);assert_eq!(text.matches(code).count(),1,"{text}");assert!(text.contains("is this intended?")&&text.contains("Repair:")&&text.contains("Intended:"));
  }
  for code in ["N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY","N_CALLER_TIME","N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE","N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","E_SECURITY_REVIEW"] {assert!(!audience(code));}
 }
}
